# Migration report

Initial import of the Pokemon assets of the Gravelmon Mod into this repository. Generated by `scripts/migrate-models` in the FrontEnd repository; do not edit by hand.

## Source

- Mod commit: `353ce04e9bafbf87600088bd1f57dd326fc3a6d2`
- Mod version: `4.0.0-Alpha.9`; first Models version line: `4.0.0`
- Catalogue (`addedByGame`): 7028 forms, commit `54b2c6279f8ac1c62125276422e9dd1f70ffd461`
- Forms with no catalogue row: their folders are placed here all the same, and no stub catalogue record is made up. The species migration lists every such form, grouped by cause, in `forms-without-catalogue-row.md` (and `.json`) next to its REPORT.md; review that list and add the rows to Gravelmon-Data.

## Reconciliation

Every file under `assets/cobblemon/bedrock`, `assets/cobblemon/textures` and `assets/gravelmon/sounds` lands in exactly one place. Total: **17195**.

| Class | Files | Placed in a form folder | Archived in `_unreferenced` | Unclaimed sprite in `<game>/.mismatched` | Stays in the Mod | Excluded |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Resolvers (`bedrock/species/**`) | 6086 | 6086 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Posers (`bedrock/pokemon/posers`) | 380 | 363 | 17 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Models (`bedrock/pokemon/models/*.geo.json`) | 386 | 367 | 19 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Animations (`bedrock/pokemon/animations`) | 379 | 307 | 72 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Pokemon textures (`textures/pokemon/**`, sprites included) | 8192 | 7302 | 36 | 854 | 0 | 0 |
| Cries (`gravelmon/sounds/pokemon/**`) | 872 | 820 | 52 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Strays (not assets: a script, a misnamed geometry) | 2 | 0 | 2 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Excluded `.bbmodel` Blockbench sources | 268 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 268 |
| Not migrated (fossils, particles, poke balls, GUI/item, shared textures) | 630 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 630 | 0 |
| **Total** | 17195 | 15245 | 198 | 854 | 630 | 268 |

## Result

- 6119 form folders in 40 games, 15245 files placed, 6004 of the folders hold their sprite
- 271 textures carry the `texture__` prefix (their name collided with the sprite or another file); 377 nested textures were joined with `__`
- 59 folders have a `uses` list (83 entries)
- 149 `credits.json` files written from the 1016 paths of 116 source files
- 6 model files added as per-form copies, 7 resolver files split out of multi-form resolvers and 15 resolvers edited in place (9 of the files because of a twin duplicate's species); the folders hold 15258 files in all
- 854 unclaimed sprites filed under `<game>/.mismatched/<file>` (not in the zip, not forms)
- 198 other files archived under `_unreferenced/assets/<path under assets/>` (move one back under `common/src/main/resources/` to restore it)

## Canonical names

Built with `--canonical-names`: every poser, model, animation and texture was given the name the submission wizard gives it, so an old form and a new one follow one system and a resubmission replaces the migrated file instead of adding a second one next to it. The wizard names the files of a form `<game>_<base>` (poser, model, animation; `<base>` is `orderedSpriteBaseName`) and keeps its textures in `textures/pokemon/<game>/<base>/`. A rename changes only the places that name the file: `poser`, `model`, `texture` and `sprites` in resolver variations, the animation group in `q.bedrock(...)` calls of posers, and the `animation.<group>.<clip>` keys of the animation file itself. A **resolver** is named by nothing, so renaming it changes no other file; but its path is part of its behaviour (Cobblemon applies resolvers of one species with the same `order` in path order), so it is renamed only when no tie that matters changes its order. Sprites are not renamed.

| Kind | Renamed | Already canonical | Left alone |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| poser | 10 | 353 | 0 |
| model | 12 | 354 | 1 |
| animation | 294 | 0 | 13 |
| texture | 121 | 1177 | 0 |
| resolver | 109 | 5940 | 34 |

Edited only to follow a rename: 127 resolvers, 295 posers, 293 animation files. `verify` undoes every rename and every edit and requires the Mod file back, byte for byte (posers, animations) or as the same JSON (resolvers).

### Left alone

Not renamed, and why. Nothing is forced: a file stays under the Mod's name when the wizard's name would be ambiguous or taken. A resubmission to such a form adds the wizard's file next to the old one.

## Decisions and judgement calls

### Models referenced by more than one form (5): one copy per form

The Mod reuses these model files for several forms. A model is never shared in this repository: every form folder owns its own copy (byte for byte the original). The form named in the model file keeps the original path; every other form gets a copy named `<game>_<folder>.geo.json`, because two folders may not install to the same path. The only content edit is in the form's resolver: the `model` of its variations now names the copy (`from` becomes `to` below). Posers, animations and textures are not models and stay shared through `uses`.

| Model | Original kept in | Copy in | Installs as | Resolver `model` rewritten |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `insurgence_steelix_mega_fire.geo.json` | insurgence/steelix_mega_fire | insurgence/steelix_mega_fire_mega_x/insurgence_steelix_mega_fire_mega_x.geo.json | `assets/cobblemon/bedrock/pokemon/models/insurgence_steelix_mega_fire_mega_x.geo.json` | `cobblemon:insurgence_steelix_mega_fire.geo` -> `cobblemon:insurgence_steelix_mega_fire_mega_x.geo` |
| `insurgence_steelix_mega_fire.geo.json` | insurgence/steelix_mega_fire | insurgence/steelix_mega_x/insurgence_steelix_mega_x.geo.json | `assets/cobblemon/bedrock/pokemon/models/insurgence_steelix_mega_x.geo.json` | `cobblemon:insurgence_steelix_mega_fire.geo` -> `cobblemon:insurgence_steelix_mega_x.geo` |
| `sage_florabri.geo.json` | sage/florabri | sage/femaleflorabri/sage_florabrifemale.geo.json | `assets/cobblemon/bedrock/pokemon/models/sage_florabrifemale.geo.json` | `cobblemon:sage_florabri.geo` -> `cobblemon:sage_florabrifemale.geo` |
| `sage_floressum.geo.json` | sage/floressum | sage/femalefloressum/sage_floressumfemale.geo.json | `assets/cobblemon/bedrock/pokemon/models/sage_floressumfemale.geo.json` | `cobblemon:sage_floressum.geo` -> `cobblemon:sage_floressumfemale.geo` |
| `sage_foliat.geo.json` | sage/foliat | sage/femalefoliat/sage_foliatfemale.geo.json | `assets/cobblemon/bedrock/pokemon/models/sage_foliatfemale.geo.json` | `cobblemon:sage_foliat.geo` -> `cobblemon:sage_foliatfemale.geo` |
| `xenoverse_pikachufemalex.geo.json` | xenoverse/femalepikachux | xenoverse/femalepikachuhoennian/xenoverse_femalepikachuhoennian.geo.json | `assets/cobblemon/bedrock/pokemon/models/xenoverse_femalepikachuhoennian.geo.json` | `cobblemon:xenoverse_pikachufemalex.geo` -> `cobblemon:xenoverse_femalepikachuhoennian.geo` |

**Resolver files that changed** (everything else in them, `order` included, is untouched). A resolver that defined several forms cannot stay in one folder once the forms own different models, so it is split: the form named in its file name keeps the path with only its own variations; each other form gets `<stem>__<folder>.json` next to it with the same `order`, which sorts right after the original (Cobblemon applies equal orders in file order), so each form's variations keep their relative order.

| Source resolver | File | Form | Variations | Model rewritten |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `additional_forms/1000_steelix_mega_fire.json` | in place: `1000_steelix_mega_fire.json` | insurgence/steelix_mega_fire | 1 | none |
| `additional_forms/1000_steelix_mega_fire.json` | new: `1000_steelix_mega_fire__steelix_mega_fire_mega_x.json` | insurgence/steelix_mega_fire_mega_x | 1 | `cobblemon:insurgence_steelix_mega_fire.geo` -> `cobblemon:insurgence_steelix_mega_fire_mega_x.geo` |
| `additional_forms/1000_steelix_mega_fire.json` | new: `1000_steelix_mega_fire__steelix_mega_x.json` | insurgence/steelix_mega_x | 1 | `cobblemon:insurgence_steelix_mega_fire.geo` -> `cobblemon:insurgence_steelix_mega_x.geo` |
| `additional_forms/1000_venusaur_delta_mega.json` | in place: `1000_venusaur_delta_mega.json` | insurgence/venusaur_delta_mega | 3 | none |
| `additional_forms/1000_venusaur_delta_mega.json` | new: `1000_venusaur_delta_mega__venusaurfemale_delta_mega.json` | insurgence/venusaurfemale_delta_mega | 3 | none |
| `additional_forms/25_pikachu_x_female.json` | in place: `25_pikachu_x_female.json` | xenoverse/femalepikachuhoennian | 1 | `cobblemon:xenoverse_pikachufemalex.geo` -> `cobblemon:xenoverse_femalepikachuhoennian.geo` |
| `additional_forms/25_pikachu_x_female.json` | new: `25_pikachu_x_female__femalepikachux.json` | xenoverse/femalepikachux | 1 | none |
| `deneb/0_rubball_base.json` | in place: `0_rubball_base.json` | deneb/rubball_deneb | 1 | none |

### Twin species names (9)

A species name that is a species file in two or more game folders is a twin. The claimant that is in the Gravelmon-Data catalogue keeps the name; every other claimant (the duplicate) gets the name suffixed with `_<addedByGame>` (`clavase` in ferran becomes `clavase_ferran`). The suffix is applied to the species name, its file, its form ids (`ferran#clavase_ferran#normal`), its spawn file and the `pokemon` of its spawn entries (each claimant keeps its own copy of the entries), its lang keys (the display text is `Clavase (Ferran)`: see `TWIN_DISPLAY_RULE`), the `name` and `actualName` of its species JSON, its resolver `species` and its Models folder (`ferran/clavase_ferran`). If neither claimant is catalogued, or several are, the one whose game sorts first keeps the name and the others are suffixed; the report lists every such decision. The Mod, Gravelmon-Data and Cobblemon are never edited.

The folder of a duplicate is named after the suffixed species, its resolvers name the suffixed species, its credits carry it. Its sprite, textures, cries, poser and model keep the install paths the Mod has (they are already per game), so nothing but the resolver changes in the game files. The species migration uses the same rule and files the duplicate under the form id `<game>#<name>_<game>#<aspects>`.

### Resolvers that define more than one form (27)

One resolver file holds the variations of several forms (usually a `female` form next to its male one). The file stays whole in the folder of the form named in its file name; the other forms list it under `uses`. These are the ones left as they are: none of their forms owns a model of its own apart from the form the file is named after. Resolvers that needed a split because of a per-form model copy are listed above, under the model copies.

### Unclaimed sprites in `<game>/.mismatched/` (854)

Flat sprites directly in `textures/pokemon/<game>/` that no resolver and no catalogue form of that game claims. They are kept, under the game they sit in, as `<game>/.mismatched/<file>`: not form folders, not errors, ignored by every validator and left out of the zip (`.gitattributes` `export-ignore`). Move one into a form folder (or delete it) when it is claimed.

- 854 sprites in 29 games
- 33 of them share their name with a form of **another game** (3 are byte-identical to that form's sprite, 30 differ); listed below
- 2 are not lower-case `.png` (`vanguard/pheromeon.PNG`, `vanguard/skartheus.PNG`)

### Game mismatches (117)

A form is listed when its resolver folder, the catalogue `addedByGame`, the species-data folder (`data/cobblemon/species/<game>/`), the texture or cry folder or its credits file do not all name the game it is filed under, or when the catalogue does not list it at all. The rule the migration applied is the same everywhere: **the folder game is the game of the form's resolver folder when it has one, else the catalogue `addedByGame`, else its texture folder**; the catalogue is cross-checked, never trusted over the Mod. The catalogue is a separate repository and some of it is stale, so each case is classed as *stale catalogue* (nothing in the Mod contradicts the folder game) or *real conflict* (the form's own files disagree).

- 117 forms are not in the catalogue at all (no row for the form): the Mod's own files agree on one game for 117 of them and disagree for 0
- 0 forms are in the catalogue but their folders and the Mod's species data name more than one game; each is explained individually below
- Credits: 0 of the 117 have a credits file (the migration splits credits per form; a credits file is filed under the identifier game, usually `pokemon`, which is not a game signal)

#### Forms the catalogue does not list

| Case | Forms | Nature | Chosen game from |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| U8: A twin duplicate: the species name is a species file in two games | 9 | stale catalogue | the twin rule (TWIN_RULE): the game folder of its own files, with the species name suffixed |
| U1: Resolver in a game folder; the catalogue lists the Pokemon in that game, but not this form | 57 | stale catalogue | the resolver folder (and so the catalogued game of the Pokemon) |
| U2: Resolver in a game folder; the catalogue does not list the Pokemon at all | 15 | stale catalogue | the resolver folder |
| U4: Resolver in `additional_forms`; the catalogue lists the Pokemon in the same game, not this form | 24 | stale catalogue | the texture folder, which agrees with the catalogued game of the Pokemon |
| U5: Resolver in `additional_forms`; the catalogue does not list the Pokemon at all | 4 | stale catalogue | the texture folder |
| U6: Resolver in `additional_forms`; the catalogue lists the Pokemon only in other games | 8 | stale catalogue | the texture folder (the game that shipped the form) |

<details><summary>U8: A twin duplicate: the species name is a species file in two games (9)</summary>

**Why it mismatches.** The same species name is a species file in two game folders, and the catalogue lists one of them. The catalogued game keeps the name; the other (this form) is the duplicate and has the species name suffixed with its game (`clavase` in ferran is `clavase_ferran`: its folder, its resolver species, its credits and, in the species migration, its form id, spawns and lang keys). Its files in the Mod still carry the old name; they are attributed to it by game.

**Nature:** stale catalogue. **Chosen:** the twin rule (TWIN_RULE): the game folder of its own files, with the species name suffixed. 0 of these forms have a credits file.

- `deneb/rubball_deneb`
- `ferran/clavase_ferran`
- `ferran/romarlord_ferran`
- `ferran/terapota_ferran`
- `nosto/budcheep_nosto`
- `sage/toxito_sage`
- `uranium/tikiki_uranium`
- `vanguard/solacor_vanguard`
- `varitas/atomix_varitas`

</details>

#### Forms in the catalogue whose folders name more than one game (0)

0 of these 0 forms are a Pokemon the Mod defines **twice, once in each of two games** (a resolver, textures and a species-data file in each game folder) while the catalogue has **one row**. Within a folder the Mod's own files agree; the conflict is between one catalogue row and two folders. The migration keeps both folders, each in its own resolver's game, so no file is lost: the folder in the catalogued game *agrees with the catalogue*, the twin is a *stale catalogue* case (no row), unless it is a leftover duplicate, which the files cannot show. A folder with no catalogue row is not a form the catalogue knows: it is still placed here, no stub record is made up for it, and the species migration lists it in its review list (`forms-without-catalogue-row.md`, written next to its REPORT.md); adding the real catalogue row is the proper fix.
